### 1a — Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1
Status: **AGREE**

### 1e — Taxable dependent care benefits from Form 2441, line 26
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"alternatives": [{"guard": {"args": [{"const": false}, {"ref": "f2441.benefits"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"param": "sales.zero"}}, {"guard": {"args": [{"const": true}, {"ref": "f2441.benefits"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"ref": "f2441.line26"}}], "op": "choose"}
```

Second:

```json
{"ref": "f2441.line26"}
```

Ruling: representation; project retained. Both enter Form 2441 line 26; the project enters zero when the filer received no dependent care benefits (f2441.benefits), Part III then not being completed, where the second reads line 26 directly. f2441 p. 1, Part I, "Did you receive dependent care benefits? No. Complete only Part II below. Yes. Complete Part III on page 2 next."; i1040gi p. 24, Line 1e, "Enter the total of your taxable dependent care benefits from Form 2441, line 26. ... But first complete Form 2441 to see if you can exclude part or all of the benefits". With no benefits, line 26 is zero either way.

### 1z — Add lines 1a through 1h
Status: **AGREE**

### 2a — Tax-exempt interest
Status: **AGREE**

### 2b — Taxable interest
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"ref": "sb.line4"}
```

Second:

```json
{"collection": "interest", "field": "interest.amount", "op": "sum_over", "where": null}
```

Second transcription's note: The text says 'Enter your total taxable interest income on line 2b'. It does not tell you to take the amount from Schedule B, so this line adds the interest from each payer. Interest adjustments that Pub. 550 covers (nominee interest, bond premium and similar) are refused through sb.unsupported.

Ruling: equivalent representation; project retained. The project enters Schedule B line 4 (total interest less excludable savings-bond interest, which the project refuses and so is zero); the second transcription totals the interest payers directly. Schedule B line 2 is the same total over the same payers (Schedule B (2025), lines 1–4), so the two agree within scope.

Project:

```json
{"args": [{"args": [{"const": true}, {"ref": "ssb.lump_sum"}], "test": "eq"}, {"args": [{"param": "sales.zero"}, {"ref": "ssb.line1"}], "test": "lt"}], "test": "all"}
```

Second:

```json
{"ref": "ssb.lump_sum"}
```

Ruling: representation; project retained. The election is for a social security benefit payment for an earlier year, so it can only apply to a filer with benefits; the project tests benefits first (the 'all' stops there) so a filer without benefits is never asked the lump-sum question, and for a filer with benefits the box is the answer itself.

Second transcription's note: Line 16 also includes tax from Forms 8814 and 4972, section 962 tax, education credit recapture, Form 8621 tax and Form 8978 amounts. Those are refused through scope.other_line16, so this line holds only the tax on taxable income. The worksheet is chosen as the Schedule D Tax Worksheet instructions describe.

Ruling: scope and representation; project retained. As the earlier ruling on this line: Form 8814, Form 4972 and the other line 16 amounts are refused through scope.other_line16, and the second selects the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet by line 3a or QDCG line 3 more than zero, which agrees with the project's guard (Schedule D line 17, or capital gain distributions when no Schedule D is needed). The only change from that response is that the second writes Schedule D line 17 out as lines 15 and 16 both above zero, which is sd.line17's definition (f1040sd p. 2, line 17, "Are lines 15 and 16 both gains?"). Both apply i1040sd p. 15, "Don’t use the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet or this worksheet to figure your tax if: Line 15 or line 16 of Schedule D is zero or less and you have no qualified dividends on Form 1040, 1040-SR, or 1040-NR, line 3a; or Form 1040, 1040-SR, or 1040-NR, line 15, is zero or less".

Second transcription's note: Line 27c lets the filer decline the EIC. This line treats a decline as no credit claimed, so line 27a is zero. Otherwise it is the worksheet credit.

Ruling: scope; project retained. As ruled before on this line: the second adds the filer's own choice not to claim the EIC (its input f1040.h_eic_decline, zero when checked), which the project does not ask (TASKS.md T-027); i1040gi p. 43, Line 27c, "Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a". Where the filer claims it, the second's line is the project's expression unchanged.

### 27b — Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)
Status: **AGREE**

### 27c — If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here
Status: **DISAGREE**

Second transcription's note: The instructions say to check the box if you do not want the EIC, or if the line 27a instructions tell you to (eic.check_27c).

Ruling: scope; project retained. As ruled before on this line: the second's box is the project's six conditions (the line 27a STOPs that say to check line 27c) or the filer's own choice not to claim the EIC, which the project does not ask (TASKS.md T-027); i1040gi p. 43, Line 27c, "Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a".

### Dependents (separated spouses) — Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the las
Status: **AGREE**

### 28 — Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812
Status: **AGREE**

### 29 — American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8
Status: **AGREE**

### 30 — Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13
Status: **AGREE**

### 31 — Amount from Schedule 3, line 15
Status: **AGREE**

### 32 — Add lines 27a, 28, 29, 30, and 31. These are your total other payments and refundable cred
Status: **AGREE**

### 33 — Add lines 25d, 26, and 32. These are your total payments
Status: **AGREE**

### 34 — If line 33 is more than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 33. This is the amount you ove
Status: **AGREE**

### 37 — Subtract line 33 from line 24. This is the amount you owe
Status: **AGREE**

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: f1040.line1a
    printed: "1a"
    label: "Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box1}
    note: "The instructions say to enter the total from Form(s) W-2, box 1, and on a joint return to include the spouse's. person.box1 holds each person's W-2 box 1 total, so this line adds it over the people on the return."

  - id: f1040.line1e
    printed: "1e"
    label: "Taxable dependent care benefits from Form 2441, line 26"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f2441.line26}

  - id: f1040.line1z
    printed: "1z"
    label: "Add lines 1a through 1h"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line1a, f1040.line1e]}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "The tool does not compute lines 1b, 1c, 1d, 1f, 1g or 1h (household wages not on a W-2, unreported tips, Medicaid waiver payments, adoption benefits, Form 8919 wages and other earned income). It refuses those cases through scope.other_income, so only 1a and 1e are added here."

  - id: f1040.line2a
    printed: "2a"
    label: "Tax-exempt interest"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.tax_exempt_interest, div.box12]}
    note: "The line 2a instructions say to also include exempt-interest dividends from Form 1099-DIV, box 12. The supplied input covers only Form 1099-INT and Form 1099-OID, so div.box12 is added to it."

  - id: f1040.line2b
    printed: "2b"
    label: "Taxable interest"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: interest, field: interest.amount}
    refuses: [sb.unsupported]
    note: "The text says 'Enter your total taxable interest income on line 2b'. It does not tell you to take the amount from Schedule B, so this line adds the interest from each payer. Interest adjustments that Pub. 550 covers (nominee interest, bond premium and similar) are refused through sb.unsupported."

  - id: f1040.line3b
    printed: "3b"
    label: "Ordinary dividends"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sb.ordinary_dividends}

  - id: f1040.line4a
    printed: "4a"
    label: "IRA distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.amount_a
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}

  - id: f1040.line4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "IRA distributions, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}

  - id: f1040.h_ira_rollovers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of IRA distributions rolled over in part or in full (Exception 1)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}

  - id: f1040.line4c
    printed: "4c"
    label: "Check if (box 1) Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1040.h_ira_rollovers, {const: 0}]}

  - id: f1040.line5a
    printed: "5a"
    label: "Pensions and annuities"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.amount_a
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}

  - id: f1040.line5b
    printed: "5b"
    label: "Pensions and annuities, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}

  - id: f1040.h_plan_rollovers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of pension or annuity distributions with a rollover, including a direct rollover"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}

  - id: f1040.line5c
    printed: "5c"
    label: "Check if (box 1) Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1040.h_plan_rollovers, {const: 0}]}

  - id: f1040.line6a
    printed: "6a"
    label: "Social security benefits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.line1}

  - id: f1040.line6b
    printed: "6b"
    label: "Social security benefits, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.line18}
    refuses: [ssb.pub915, ssb.lump_sum, ssb.terror_disability]

  - id: f1040.line6c
    printed: "6c"
    label: "If you elect to use the lump-sum election method, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.lump_sum}

  - id: f1040.line6d
    printed: "6d"
    label: "If you are married filing separately and lived apart from your spouse the entire year, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [ssb.lived_apart, {const: true}]}

  - id: f1040.line7a
    printed: "7a"
    label: "Capital gain or (loss). Attach Schedule D if required"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sd.to1040}

  - id: f1040.line8
    printed: "8"
    label: "Additional income from Schedule 1, line 10"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s1.line10}

  - id: f1040.line9
    printed: "9"
    label: "Add lines 1z, 2b, 3b, 4b, 5b, 6b, 7a, and 8. This is your total income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line1z, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, f1040.line4b, f1040.line5b, f1040.line6b, f1040.line7a, f1040.line8]}

  - id: f1040.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "Adjustments to income from Schedule 1, line 26"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s1.line26}

  - id: f1040.line11a
    printed: "11a"
    label: "Subtract line 10 from line 9. This is your adjusted gross income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1040.line9, take: f1040.line10}

  - id: f1040.line11b
    printed: "11b"
    label: "Amount from line 11a (adjusted gross income)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11a}

  - id: f1040.line12e
    printed: "12e"
    label: "Standard deduction or itemized deductions (from Schedule A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sa.deduction}
    refuses: [sa.dependent]
    note: "The tool does not compute the Standard Deduction Worksheet for Dependents (line 12a) or the dual-status alien case (line 12c). Both are refused through sa.dependent."

  - id: f1040.line13a
    printed: "13a"
    label: "Qualified business income deduction from Form 8995 or Form 8995-A"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: qbi.deduction}
    refuses: [qbi.unsupported]

  - id: f1040.line13b
    printed: "13b"
    label: "Additional deductions from Schedule 1-A, line 38"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s1a.line38}

  - id: f1040.line14
    printed: "14"
    label: "Add lines 12e, 13a, and 13b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line12e, f1040.line13a, f1040.line13b]}

  - id: f1040.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Subtract line 14 from line 11b. If zero or less, enter -0-. This is your taxable income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line11b, take: f1040.line14}

  - id: f1040.h_tax_regular
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tax on line 15 using the Tax Table or Tax Computation Worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: tax_on_amount, bind: {amount: f1040.line15, status: f1040.status}, line: tax}}

  - id: f1040.h_tax_exception
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception: don't use the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet or the Schedule D Tax Worksheet (line 15 is zero or less, or Schedule D line 15 or 16 is zero or less and there are no qualified dividends)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, le: [f1040.line15, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, le: [qdcg.line3, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.line3a, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The exception says 'Line 15 or line 16 of Schedule D is zero or less'. That wording does not cover a filer who does not file Schedule D. This line uses qdcg.line3 instead: for a Schedule D filer it is the smaller of Schedule D lines 15 and 16, or zero if either is a loss; for anyone else it is line 7a. The test is that qdcg.line3 is zero or less."

  - id: f1040.h_use_sdtw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Complete the Schedule D Tax Worksheet (Schedule D line 18 or 19 more than zero and lines 15 and 16 gains, or Form 4952 filed with an amount on line 4g)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, gt: [sd.line18, {const: 0}]}
                - {op: test, gt: [sd.line19, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [sd.line15, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [sd.line16, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [sd.filing4952, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [f4952.line4g, {const: 0}]}

  - id: f1040.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Tax (see instructions). Check if any from Form(s): 1 8814 2 4972 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_tax_exception, {const: true}]}
          then: f1040.h_tax_regular
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_tax_exception, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_sdtw, {const: true}]}
          then: sdtw.line47
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_tax_exception, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_sdtw, {const: false}]}
          then: qdcg.line25
    refuses: [scope.other_line16]
    note: "Line 16 also includes tax from Forms 8814 and 4972, section 962 tax, education credit recapture, Form 8621 tax and Form 8978 amounts. Those are refused through scope.other_line16, so this line holds only the tax on taxable income. The worksheet is chosen as the Schedule D Tax Worksheet instructions describe."

  - id: f1040.line17
    printed: "17"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 2, line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line3}
    refuses: [s2.amt, s2.other]

  - id: f1040.line18
    printed: "18"
    label: "Add lines 16 and 17"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line16, f1040.line17]}

  - id: f1040.line19
    printed: "19"
    label: "Child tax credit or credit for other dependents from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line14}

  - id: f1040.line20
    printed: "20"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s3.line8}
    refuses: [scope.credits]

  - id: f1040.line21
    printed: "21"
    label: "Add lines 19 and 20"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line19, f1040.line20]}

  - id: f1040.line22
    printed: "22"
    label: "Subtract line 21 from line 18. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line18, take: f1040.line21}

  - id: f1040.line23
    printed: "23"
    label: "Other taxes, including self-employment tax, from Schedule 2, line 21"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line21}
    refuses: [s2.other]

  - id: f1040.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Add lines 22 and 23. This is your total tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line22, f1040.line23]}

  - id: f1040.line25a
    printed: "25a"
    label: "Federal income tax withheld from Form(s) W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box2}

  - id: f1040.line25d
    printed: "25d"
    label: "Add lines 25a through 25c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line25a, f1040.line25b, f1040.line25c]}

  - id: f1040.h_eic_decline
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do you not want to claim the earned income credit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Form 1040, line 27c: If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here (interview question)"}

  - id: f1040.line27a
    printed: "27a"
    label: "Earned income credit (EIC)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_eic_decline, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_eic_decline, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.credit
    note: "Line 27c lets the filer decline the EIC. This line treats a decline as no credit claimed, so line 27a is zero. Otherwise it is the worksheet credit."

  - id: f1040.line27b
    printed: "27b"
    label: "Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.clergy}

  - id: f1040.line27c
    printed: "27c"
    label: "If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_eic_decline, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: true}]}
    note: "The instructions say to check the box if you do not want the EIC, or if the line 27a instructions tell you to (eic.check_27c)."

  - id: "f1040.lineDependents (separated spouses)"
    printed: "Dependents (separated spouses)"
    label: "Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the last 6 months of 2025, or you are legally separated according to your state law under a written separation agreement or a decree of separate maintenance and you did not live in the same household as your spouse at the end of 2025."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.separated_box}
    note: "The id follows the requested f1040.line<printed> pattern. The box is checked as EIC Step 3, question 6 and 'Special rule for separated spouses' direct, and eic.separated_box records that."

  - id: f1040.line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line27}

  - id: f1040.line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]
    note: "The tool does not compute Form 8863. A claim for the credit is refused through scope.credits, so the only case computed is no credit."

  - id: f1040.line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]
    note: "The tool does not compute Form 8839. A claim for the credit is refused through scope.credits, so the only case computed is no credit."

  - id: f1040.line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s3.line15}

  - id: f1040.line32
    printed: "32"
    label: "Add lines 27a, 28, 29, 30, and 31. These are your total other payments and refundable credits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line27a, f1040.line28, f1040.line29, f1040.line30, f1040.line31]}

  - id: f1040.line33
    printed: "33"
    label: "Add lines 25d, 26, and 32. These are your total payments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line25d, f1040.line26, f1040.line32]}

  - id: f1040.line34
    printed: "34"
    label: "If line 33 is more than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 33. This is the amount you overpaid"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line33, take: f1040.line24}
    note: "The line is entered only if line 33 is more than line 24. Otherwise it is left blank, which is written here as zero."

  - id: f1040.line37
    printed: "37"
    label: "Subtract line 33 from line 24. This is the amount you owe"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line24, take: f1040.line33}
    note: "The printed line does not say what to do when line 33 is more than line 24. That case is an overpayment reported on line 34, so this line is zero then. The estimated tax penalty on line 38 is not included."
```
```
